# 2026-11-02 - Session 90 (iteration 99: the packages trio rides one ladder and wave skip gets a compounding law)

## Asked

The Utility track was the last uncited block in the Workshop sitemap. Two questions: do the three `Recovery Package` lines
share a price ladder the way Â§1.8/Â§1.10 suggested, and do the two prose-only pages (`wave-skip`, `wave-accelerator`) carry
printed numbers that change the run model?

## Sources

Tower Hub `wiki/workshop/{recovery-packages, wave-skip, wave-accelerator, more-round-stats}` (**2026-11-01**;
`more-round-stats` **2026-10-13**). Parsed with `tools/workshop-line-census.ps1` (now also accepting the
`Level | Value | Coins | Total Coins` header variant) and `tools/hub-nav.ps1`.

## What was established

**A. One ladder, three value laws (Â§1.12).** The three Packages lines print *identical* checkpoint prices and running totals
(L1 1.00 K; L10 20.90 K / 59.12 K; L20 334.63 K / 1.52 M; L30 1.66 M / 10.92 M; L40 5.12 M / 44.23 M; L50 12.18 M /
130.57 M; L60 25.40 M / 321.28 M) over three different caps: `Package Chance` **60** levels â†’ **30.00 %** for **321.28 M**
(`6 + 0.4Â·L` âœ“), `Recovery Amount` **300** levels â†’ **134.00 %** for **1.33 T** (`14 + 0.4Â·L` âœ“), `Max Recovery` **500**
levels â†’ **16.50x** for **15.77 T** (`1.50 + 0.03Â·L` âœ“). Prefix-sum verified 5/5 on the dense table (Î£ **321.27 M** vs the
printed **321.28 M**). So the ranking is set by the **value step and the depth**: at level 1 all three cost **2 500 coins per
printed percentage point** (the cheapest pp in the layer), the second 10-level band is **24.7Ã—** the first, and averaged to
their caps `Package Chance` costs **13.61 M**/pp while `Recovery Amount` costs **11.12 B**/pp (817Ã—) purely from its 300-level
depth â‡’ cap `Package Chance`, hold `Recovery Amount` at ~L30â€“L50, defer `Max Recovery` (1.053 B per Ã—1.00).

**B. `Packages +` closes one Â§6.4 row.** Printed to its cap: `1 + 0.01Â·L` â‡’ **Ã—7.00** at **600** levels âœ“ (the same law as
Â§6.4's twelve 600-level rows), bill **441.24 s** coins â€” i.e. **36 %** of Â§6.4's entire Utility-track bill (7.598 S) is this
one line.

**C. `Wave Skip` is a Ã—1.1-per-wave compounding income line, with printed ladders.** Income of the last completed wave
(coins, cash, cells, UW and Golden Bot included) is multiplied by **`1.1^n`** for `n` chained skips â‡’
n = 2 **1.21**, 3 **1.331**, 5 **1.611**, 10 **2.594** âœ“. Skip chance by star **1â€“7** = **9 / 10 / 11 / 13 / 15 / 17 / 19 %**
(hand-set +1, +1, +2, +2, +2, +2 â€” the same shape T010 recorded for the module layer). The mastery lab is
**`10 % + 5 %Â·L` â‡’ 55 %** at **max level 9** âœ“, unlocked for **1 000** stones; the card's own mastery
(â€œSpawn Acceleratorâ€) is also **1 000** stones. Skipped Boss waves still roll reroll-shard and module chances. Costs:
**time-based mechanics do not accelerate**, so each skip is effectively one Energy Shield charge lost (Demon Mode instead
persists over more waves); `Free Upgrades` are rolled per wave *completed* after a skip and both `Enemy Level Skip` lines can
fire up to the number of waves skipped.

**D. `Wave Accelerator` is a cooldown-only card** (the 9-s half of T002's `26 + 9` s wave clock), which is also why the page
says it makes Energy Shield charge faster; the spawn schedule itself only compresses with its 1 000-stone mastery.

**E. `More Round Stats` priced.** **1**-level lab, **T1 W250** gate, **250 000** coins, **0d 12h 0m**; unlocks Battle
History, which keeps the last **20** runs.

## Next open question

Does the mastery lab's `10 % + 5 %Â·L` double-skip chance apply per wave (so the expected skipped-wave count is a geometric
series with ratio 0.55) or once per chain? The page prints only the chance, so the two readings differ by a factor of
~2.2 in the `1.1^n` tail â€” a device observation of one long skip chain settles it. Device reads unchanged: **H060**,
**H050**, **H058**, **H059**, the T010 Â§8 skews, T024 Â§6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew.

## Verdict

**T020 Rev 12.** The Workshop layer is now fully priced across all three tracks: the Packages trio's shared ladder gives the
cheapest pp in the game (**2 500 coins** at level 1) with a **24.7Ã—** second-band knee, `Packages +` is a printed
**441.24 s** line, and `Wave Skip` now carries an explicit compounding law (**1.1^n**) that T002's phase model and T006's
cell ledger can use directly. **Next:** the `wiki/uw/ultimate-weapons` index page's unparsed tables, then a second pass over
`wiki/system/*` for anything still uncited.

# 2026-11-02 - Session 91 (iteration 100: the twenty-seven workshop lines are priced and the five damage lines ride one ladder)

## Asked

The knee table in T008 §3 covered only six of the nine pages' **27** basic-stat lines (GT's three, the three `−10 s`
cooldown rows, two GT block rows). Two questions: what do the remaining 21 lines cost block by block, and is there a law
behind the shapes the nine `wiki/uw/<name>` tables print?

## Sources

Tower Hub's nine `wiki/uw/{black-hole, chain-lightning, chrono-field, death-wave, golden-tower, inner-land-mines,
poison-swamp, smart-missiles, spotlight}` pages (last verified **2026-11-01**, re-pulled **2026-11-02** with
`tools/fetch-hub.ps1`). New parser `tools/uw-track-knees.ps1`: it takes every `(value, cost)` column pair to its last
numeric cell, re-adds it against the page's own `Total` cell, prints the per-block cost and marginal stones-per-unit, and
groups ladders that agree cell for cell.

## What was established

**A. The ledger closes at line level (27/27).** Every one of the 27 `Cost` columns sums exactly to its page's printed
`Total` cell, and the 27 sums re-add to §2's **244 542**. The nine page totals are therefore backed by 27 independent
column checks instead of nine.

**B. Six lines are one arithmetic progression each** (`Σ = n/2 × (2c₁ + (n−1)d)`): BH Cooldown (c₁ **10**, d **18**,
n 15 ⇒ 7.5 × 272 = **2 040** ✓), Chain Lightning `Chance` and Poison Swamp `Cooldown` (both c₁ **8**, d **18**, n 15 ⇒
7.5 × 268 = **2 010** ✓ — the *same* ladder twice), ILM Cooldown (c₁ 8, d **16**, n 15 ⇒ 7.5 × 240 = **1 800** ✓),
CF Duration (c₁ 5, d **9**, n 35 ⇒ 17.5 × 316 = **5 530** ✓) and CF Cooldown (c₁ 10, d **21**, n 12 ⇒ 6 × 251 =
**1 506** ✓). Two printed cells price such a line completely.

**C. The five `×damage` lines are one ladder** (CL, DW, ILM, PS, SM agree for at least their first **17** cells:
`5 11 17 23 29 35 41 47 53 61 71 84 100 120 144 174 210`, i.e. **+6 arithmetic for nine levels**, Σ 261), so each costs
exactly **85 / 322 / 841** stones at level **5 / 10 / 15** — five times ✓. ILM, PS and SM also print an identical
36-cell *value* row (`10.00× … 9 219.00×`). Consequence: these five are the only lines that get **cheaper** with depth
(**×0.24 … ×0.40** against level 1), because their value row is itself geometric with ratio ≈ 1.19 versus the cost
row's ≈ 1.2 — the price per ×1 falls from **5** to ≈ **1.7–2.0**.

**D. Two price shapes, and they split the buying order.** The other 22 lines are "arithmetic head + geometric tail" and
degrade from **×2.8** (ILM Quantity) to **×580** (Spotlight Bonus): GT Multiplier **×400**, SM Quantity **×375**, GT
Duration **×350**, Spotlight Angle **×317**, DW Cooldown **×187**. Their heads are tiny in absolute terms (GT
Multiplier's `5, 13` at +8 is **18** stones of its 8 434; Spotlight Bonus's `5, 13, 21` is **39** of 20 071), so almost
every line's whole price sits in its tail. Cheapest per unit at level 1: the `−10 s` cooldown lines (**0.8**–**1**
stone per second), PS Cooldown **1.6**, then **2**–**6.25** for the duration / slow / size / angle / damage lines, while
the `Quantity` lines cost **30**–**375** per extra projectile ⇒ quantity lines are level-1 purchases.

**E. Three budget rows + the package.** First 5 levels of all 27 lines = **11 461** stones = **4.69 %** of 244 542;
first 10 = **25 045** (**10.2 %**); first 15 = **49 194** (**20.1 %**). Per page (first 5): BH 441 · CL 960 · CF 635 ·
DW 3 235 · GT 460 · ILM 710 · PS 525 · SM 526 · Spotlight 3 969 ✓ re-add to 11 461. The five `Quantity` lines alone take
**7 996** of that = **70 %** for 3–5 levels each. The knee-first package over all 27 lines (§3.①) is **8 865** stones =
**3.63 %** of the bill, and it contains §4's triple-sync package because GT 910 + BH 910 + DW 800 = **2 620** ✓ — the
parser's L10 column reproduces §3's hand-read blocks (910 / 910 / 800 / 534 / 1 076) exactly.

## Next open question

Whether the `Quantity` lines' value is linear per projectile (then 30–375 stones per projectile is a fair price and they
rank *above* the ×damage tails) or capped by how many enemies are on screen — the nine pages print only the value
column. A device read of the projectile count on one kill (1# vs 2# on the same wave) settles it. Device reads
unchanged: **H060**, **H050**, **H058**, **H059**, the T010 §8 skews, T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew.

## Verdict

**T008 Rev 9.** The UW stone block is priced at line level: **27/27** column re-adds, six arithmetic closed forms, one
shared ladder behind five damage lines (85 / 322 / 841), and two price shapes that decide the tap order — push the
`×damage` lines deep (they never get worse than ≈ 2 stones per ×1), stop everything else at its first block. The whole
first-block layer is **11 461** stones (**4.69 %** of the maxed bill), so a sub-T11 account can hold essentially all the
practical UW value inside about 13 months of free stone income. **Next:** the `wiki/uw/ultimate-weapons` index page's
unparsed tables, then a second pass over `wiki/system/*` for anything still uncited.

# 2026-11-02 - Session 92 (iteration 101: the index page prints the whole plus layer and the value rows get a second source)

## Asked

The `wiki/uw/ultimate-weapons` index page is the one UW page whose tables had never been parsed (iterations 73–82 used
only the nine per-weapon pages). Two questions: does it price the UW+ layer independently of those nine pages, and do
its `Min`/`Max` effect columns confirm the 27 value rows of T008 §3.①?

## Sources

Tower Hub `wiki/uw/ultimate-weapons` (4 data tables, last verified **2026-11-01**, re-pulled **2026-11-02** with
`tools/fetch-hub.ps1`). Cross-checked against the nine per-weapon payloads via `tools/uw-track-knees.ps1`, which now
also (a) checks the value-row law `n = (max − min) / step`, and (b) holds the index page's printed min/max table as a
second source.

## What was established

**A. The three consolidated cost rows are §1.④'s families, cell for cell (42/42).** The page prints three
`Base Scaling` rows under headers that state their totals: `300 … 4300` under `Total Cost: 23800`, `300 … 4150` under
`Total Cost: 18570`, `400 … 2700` under `Total Cost: 19050`. Rebuilt from §1.④'s step lists, each row matches its
family for all **14** cells (A from `60 70 80 110 130 150 200 250 300 400 550 700 850`, B from
`100 110 120 130 140 150 150 200 200 250 250 250 250 300`, C from `75 100 125 125 200 225 300 350 400 450 500 550 600`).
Column sums: **23 800** (header ✓), **20 070** (header prints **18 570**), **19 050** (header ✓).

**B. The A / A′ gap is printed on one page.** The middle row's 14 cells sum to **20 070** = GT+'s bill while its header
says **18 570** = ILM+'s bill, and 20 070 − 18 570 = **1 500** — exactly §1.④'s A − A′ tail delta (50 / 200 / 450 / 800
at levels 11…14). So the index page merges the two 300-start trees under one header; its rows also stop at level 14, so
Light Range's levels 15–19 (five `+300` steps = 18 000, 19 050 → **37 050** ✓) exist only on `wiki/uw/spotlight`. Its
prose "each ability can be upgraded ten times" contradicts its own 14-cell rows and the nine caps
**11 / 13 / 14 / 19** (H033) ⇒ tables over prose.

**C. Two printed totals that were prose-only before.** The slot table now carries its own `Total` cell **9705** (the
nine cells 5 … 3 000 re-add ✓), and the UW+ `Unlock` row repeats **500 … 3 800** (Σ **14 650** ✓). The page also fixes
the plus-layer rules: opens after all nine UWs, any order, each purchase raises the next price, and newly unlocked
abilities **start at level 0** (which is why §1.④'s value rows begin above zero).

**D. All 27 value rows are double-sourced, and depth becomes a one-line law (§3.①, §1.⑥).** The effect table's
`Min`/`Max` columns agree with the parsed per-weapon value rows **27/27**, and it prints the *panel's* own line names —
GT's first line is `Bonus` (not "Multiplier"), CF's second is `Speed Reduction`, GT's maxed cooldown reads **1m 40s**
= 100 s. For the 22 arithmetic rows, `levels = (max − min) ÷ step` reproduces the printed depth **22/22** (GT `Bonus`
(21.0 − 5.0)/0.8 = 20, GT `Cooldown` (300 − 100)/10 = 20, SM `Cooldown` (180 − 20)/10 = 16, PS `Cooldown`
(125 − 50)/5 = 15, CL `Chance` (27.5 − 5.0)/1.5 = 15); only the five `×damage` rows are geometric and need their cells.
Practical shape: **two reads on the workshop panel give a line's depth, and §3.① then gives its block prices.**

**E. Extra printed facts.** Full UW damage formula (the basis of §1.③'s shares), **40 toggles per run**, the 3-offer
persistence rule, "Chain Lightning has no cooldown and rolls on every projectile hit" ✓ consistent with its three
lines (`Damage / Quantity / Chance`, no Cooldown row), the Ultimate Crit card applying to damage-dealing UWs "but not
to percentage-based damage", and **Smite's `Max hits: 100/enemy`**.

## Next open question

The index page prints Light Range only to **0.15** (14 cells) while `wiki/uw/spotlight` runs to **0.20** (19 levels):
is the 15-level row just truncated, or does the panel stop listing values after level 14 while prices keep rising? One
panel read of `Light Range` at level 15 settles it. Device reads unchanged: **H060**, **H050**, **H058**, **H059**, the
T010 §8 skews, T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew.

## Verdict

**T008 Rev 10.** The UW+ layer now has **two independent prints** of every price (nine per-ability tables + the index
page's three consolidated rows, 42/42 cells), the basic-stat layer has two prints of every value range (27/27), and
depth is a one-line law for 22 of the 27 lines. For the plan nothing changes numerically — which is the point: the
stone bill (**9 705 + 14 650 + 244 542 + 179 240 = 448 137**) is now over-determined, so the tap order in §3.① and §4
can be trusted. **Next:** a second pass over `wiki/system/*` for anything still uncited, then the per-card pages whose
ladders are still unparsed.

# 2026-11-02 - Session 93 (iteration 102: the 99-row discount ladder folds into 20 blocks and each tab gets its own stop level)

## Asked

`wiki/system/workshop-defense-discount` is the only page that prints a tab-discount lab's **whole 99-row** table. Three
questions: does its own column sum match the **111 682 359** used since Rev 9, does its `Time` column read per-level like
the other lab tables (H050), and can the stop level be obtained at *block* granularity so the three tabs can be compared
without re-solving the per-level crossover each time?

## Sources

Tower Hub `wiki/system/workshop-defense-discount` (fetched 2026-11-02, `Level | Time | Cost | Value`, 99 rows, gate
printed **T2 W50**), plus the printed totals already catalogued in T020 §1.2 / §1.4 (`111 683 349` and the `Starting
Cash` ladder). New parsers: `tools/lab-fourcol-parse.ps1` (rows, checkpoints, coins-per-pp), `tools/lab-time-shape.ps1`
(per-level vs cumulative test), `tools/lab-payback.ps1` (block economics against a track bill), `tools/lab-block-tail.ps1`
(cumulative at every 5-level mark, the 96–99 block, the 990 identity), `tools/lab-shape.ps1` (growth ratios, the Utility
four-line block ladder).

## What was established

**A. The page closes on itself.** `Value` = `0.50 % × n` for all 99 rows (`0.50%` → `49.5%`), last `Cost` =
**4 190 609**, and the **column sums to 111 682 359** ✓ §6.5's bill; the gate reads **T2 W50** ✓ §6.5's table.

**B. Per-level `Time`, now on this ladder too.** The 99 cells sum to **2 080 811 min = 1 445.0 d** = the ≈1 445 d the
fandom prints as the per-lab bill of the five T1-W30 labs, whereas the last cell alone is **70 618 min = 49.0 d**
(Σ/last = **29.47**) ⇒ the per-level reading of H050 reproduces a *second* page's total.

**C. A new identity among the five T1-W30-family labs.** Income labs **111 683 349** − discount ladder **111 682 359** =
**990 = 99 × 10** exactly ⇒ §1.4's “10 coins under the income ladder” is a **per-level** offset that survives to L99, not
a first-rows artifact. One ladder therefore prices five labs (four income at +10/level, `Starting Cash` and, identically,
the three tab-discount labs).

**D. 20 five-level blocks + the payback rule.** Block prices **1 449 · 17 303 · 68 931 · 174 633 · 350 702 · 612 218 ·
973 408 · 1 447 847 · 2 048 578 · 2 788 207 · 3 678 974 · 4 732 789 · 5 961 283 · 7 375 828 · 8 987 577 · 10 807 466 ·
12 846 249 · 15 114 506 · 17 622 654 · 16 071 757** (blocks 1–19 = **95 610 602**, tail = **16 071 757**, sum ✓
111 682 359). A block buys **+2.5 pp**, so it pays back iff the discounted bill `B ≥ 40 × price` (50 × for the 4-level
tail): thresholds **6 985 320 / 14 028 080 / 24 488 720 / 38 936 320 / 57 913 880 / 81 943 120 / 111 528 280 …**

**E. The three tabs now have three different stop levels** (§6.5.1): Defense `B = 10.767 M` ⇒ last paying block
L16–20 (**1.54×**) ⇒ **L20**; Attack `B = 22.88 M` ⇒ L21–25 (**1.63×**) ⇒ **L25**; Utility four currency lines
`B = 34.29 M` ⇒ L26–30 (**1.40×**) ⇒ **L30** ✓ the same stop the per-level rule gave; Utility with the `Packages` trio
`B = 17.42 T` ⇒ **all 99** (even the tail block returns **2.17 × 10⁴×**). Whole-lab returns: **0.048 / 0.101 / 0.152 /
77 209×**. Shape: per-level price ratio **2.25 → 1.22 → 1.06 → 1.03** over L1→5 / 10→20 / 40→50 / 90→99, so beyond L20
the ladder is ≈ **×1.03 per level** = ×1.16–1.24 per block.

## Next open question

The block rule uses the *original* bill while §6.5's per-level rule uses the *remaining* bill; they agree at every stop
level printed above, but a device read that shows the discount applied **twice** on the same purchase (tab discount and
`Free Upgrades` percentages) would say the two layers are not simply multiplicative. Device reads unchanged: **H060**,
**H050**, **H058**, **H059**, T010 §8 skews, T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew, and whether Light Range's panel
lists values past level 14.

## Verdict

**T020 Rev 13.** §6.5.1 turns a 99-row table into 20 block prices and one comparison (`B ≥ 40 × price`), which is the
form a player can actually use on the phone: read the tab's own `Coins to max`-style total once, then keep buying blocks
while that number clears the threshold. Both printed closes (column sum, 1 445.0 d) and the 990 identity mean the whole
T1-W30 lab family is now one ladder with a 10-coin offset. **Next:** the remaining uncited `wiki/system/*` pages
(`the-tower`, `challenges`, `seasons`, `profile`), then the per-card ladders still unparsed.

# 2026-11-02 - Session 94 (iteration 103: the Super Tower Bonus lab is a fourth clock shape and prices the card product to 513)

## Asked

`wiki/system/*` had one numeric page left un-transcribed (`the-tower`, whose single data table no earlier iteration had
parsed). Two questions: does its ladder obey one of the three lab clock shapes already catalogued in T006, and does its
value column have a closed form from the printed rows alone?

## Sources

Tower Hub `wiki/system/the-tower` (**1 data table**, last verified 2026-11-01, re-pulled 2026-11-02) and
`wiki/system/content-creator-page` (2026-11-01). New parser `tools/stb-lab.ps1` (value law, price ratios and geometric
fit, Σ10, the two 5-level blocks, both readings of the `Time` column, the 30-level extrapolation, the card products). The
`challenges` page was already recorded as a link-only index page and `seasons` / `profile` are cited by T016 / T013.

## What was established

**A. The lab is `Super Tower Bonus`, and its printed rows are new.** Gate **T8 W300** ✓ T019's tier-8 row, **30 levels**
✓ T006's census row (three prints agree on the cap), and the table itself shows **10 of 30** rows: L1 **2.00 B** coins /
**0d 5h 0m** / **1.03** … L10 **32.14 B** / **5d 2h 32m** / **1.30**.

**B. The value column has a closed form.** `Value = 1 + 0.03·L`, exact **10/10**, so with the printed 30-level cap the line
ends at **×1.90**. Two panel reads (L1 and the level counter) give both the step and the cap.

**C. A fourth clock shape.** Price ratios are **1.500** once, then **1.31–1.37** (fit over L2–L10 = **×1.3451/level**) —
neither the ×1.5 stat-line law nor the ×2.25 Echo law applies, so B-scale lines need their own fit. Σ10 = **115.94 B**
coins, and in the units a player buys: **L1–5 = 21.37 B** for +0.15 and **L6–10 = 94.57 B** for +0.15 (**×4.43**) ⇒ the
knee is inside the first ten levels. Extrapolated 30-level bill ≈ **47 T** coins (last level ≈ 12.07 T) with ≈ **1 654 d**
of base slot time under the per-level reading.

**D. The `Time` column keeps the H050 ambiguity, with a one-tap test.** Cells 300 … 7 352 min: per-level ⇒ Σ10 =
**31 962 min = 22.2 d**; cumulative ⇒ **7 352 min = 5.1 d** (Σ/last = **4.35**). This table prints no `Total` row, so the
reading has to come from the panel's own `Research time` at level 1 (**5 h** ⇒ per-level).

**E. What the multiplier is worth.** The page's wording (“raises the multiplier on the `Super Tower` card”) plugs straight
into T012's printed product: `Damage` ×5 × `Demon Mode` ×6 × `Super Tower` ×9 = **270** becomes **351** at L10 and **513**
at L30 ⇒ **+81** for 115.94 B coins, **+243** for ≈ 47 T.

**F. Four taxonomy prints, all as second sources.** Workshop = the **minimum level** of the matching in-run upgrade
(T020 §1); the Lab either improves *scaling* or raises the *cap*, with `Max Interest` named as the cap case (T001 §C);
cards are slot-limited and some are locked mid-run (T012 §5); one perk every **200 rounds**, only after its lab (T011).
`content-creator-page`: creator code = **5 %** of in-app / **8 %** of Webstore revenue to the creator at no cost to the
buyer, valid **30 days**, entered in **Settings** / on the Webstore, **gone on iOS since 29.0.5**.

## Next open question

Whether the STB multiplier applies to the card's **mastery** multiplier (the ×9 inside the 270 product) or to the card's
per-level value — the products above assume the former. One before/after damage report at STB L0 vs L10 settles it.
Device reads unchanged: **H060**, **H050** (the STB L1 timer is now the easiest test), **H058**, **H059**, T010 §8 skews,
T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew, Light Range's values past level 14.

## Verdict

**T006 Rev 19.** The lab layer's fourth price/time shape is on the record with its own closed-form value law, so any
B-scale lab can be forecast from two printed rows instead of a table dump. The practical buy for a sub-T11 account is
**L1–L10 of `Super Tower Bonus` = 115.94 B coins** (block 2 already costs 4.43× block 1) for the Super Tower card's
**×1.30**. **Next:** the per-card ladders still unparsed, then a first pass over the uncited `wiki/currency/*` pages.

## Housekeeping note

`README.md` was double-encoded (the earlier patch scripts read it with ANSI `Get-Content` and wrote it back as UTF-8), so
non-ASCII cells rendered as `â†’`. `tools/fix-encoding.ps1` now inverts one layer per UTF-8 sequence (**423** repaired,
0 mojibake sequences left, 24 rows / 0 bad pipes), and `tools/patch-readme-103.ps1` reads and writes with explicit UTF-8
so the bug cannot return. `tools/check-row.ps1` / `tools/find-paren.ps1` report per-row pipe counts and paren balance.

# 2026-11-02 - Session 95 (iteration 104: the per-card pages double-source both ladders and print Coin Decay)

## Asked

The per-card pages (`wiki/card/cards-<name>`, 36 of them, none cited before this iteration) are the only pages that print a
single card's ladder on its own. Two questions: do their tables agree with the deck-wide table T012 §1 and the mastery
table §2.1, and do they print anything those two pages do not?

## Sources

Tower Hub `wiki/card/cards-{cash, coins, critical-coin, wave-skip, enemy-balance, recovery-package-chance, free-upgrades,
super-tower}` (all last verified **2026-11-01**), parsed with the new `tools/card-page-parse.ps1`; `tools/uncited-pages.ps1`
(now added) lists which sitemap entries under a wiki section still lack a citation in `docs/`.

## What was established

**A. Every card page carries two tables.** `Card Level | Effect` with **7** rows and `Mastery Level | Value` with **10**
rows (*k* = 0…9). 7 of the 8 pulled pages print both; `cards-recovery-package-chance` prints only the card ladder, so the
`Package Chance` mastery ladder remains a `card-masteries`-only print (census note).

**B. The card ladders match §1 cell for cell (8/8)** — `Cash` ×1.20 → ×2.40 (+0.20), `Coins` ×1.15 → ×1.45 (+0.05),
`Critical Coin` 15 → 33 % (+3), `Enemy Balance` ×1.30 → ×1.90 (+0.10), `Free Upgrades` 4 → 10 % (+1), and the two
**non-arithmetic** shapes reproduce exactly: `Wave Skip` 9 · 10 · 11 · 13 · 15 · 17 · 19 (+1, +1, +2 ×4) and
`Super Tower` 2.5 · 2.9 · 3.3 · 3.7 · 4.1 · 4.5 · 5 (+0.4 ×5 then +0.5). The deck table is now double-sourced.

**C. The mastery ladder has its own law, printed.** For every arithmetic mastery table **`bonus(k) = (k + 1) × s`**:
`Cash` 0.4 → 4 % (s = 0.4), `Critical Coin` 10 → 100 (10), `Enemy Balance` 6 → 60 (6), `Free Upgrades` 1 → 10 (1),
`Super Tower` −3 s → −30 s (−3), and `Coins` in ×-form as `1 + 0.03 × (k + 1)` ⇒ **level 0 is exactly one tenth of level 9**
— §2.1's 10 % law, re-derived from a second page type. `Wave Skip` (`10 + 5k`, 10/55 = 2/11) is again the only break.

**D. Stone costs are printed per page** and all seven sit inside §2.1's four buckets: **500** (`Cash`, `Free Upgrades`),
**1 000** (`Critical Coin`, `Enemy Balance`, `Wave Skip`, `Super Tower`), **1 250** (`Coins`) ⇒ the **27 500** census has a
second print and only `Cells` (= 1 000 by the fandom-total reconciliation) stays unprinted.

**E. Three mechanic prints that no earlier entry carried**: **Coin Decay** — “enemies that live **3 waves** or more drop
**half** coins”; the cosmetic stack in per-item form (skins **+0.4 %**, backgrounds **+0.8 %**, menu skins / profile
banners / the three shop songs / Guardian skins **+0.6 %** each, “they add up … and the total multiplies the whole coin
bonus” ✓ the same **+95.2 pp ⇒ ×1.952**); and the `Cash` page's definition set (cash resets each round, coins are
permanent, **Cash Bonus multiplies all cash incl. the wave bonus**).

**F. H058 gains prose support for reading (A).** The `Coins` page says the mastery “**increases the card's stat
multiplier**, making the coin bonus stronger than the base card alone” — a multiplier on the card's multiplier. Two of the
eight pages use that wording, none uses “the part above ×1.00”; the hypothesis stays open until the numeric read.

## Next open question

Whether the mastery tables’ unit-by-family pattern (%) / (×) / (s) hides a different *level count* for some masteries: the
pulled pages all print 10 rows, but the 29 unpulled card pages are untested — one more pull would turn the census into a
law. Device reads unchanged: **H060**, **H050**, **H058** (now with two candidate panel reads), **H059**, T010 §8 skews,
T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 % `Defense %` skew, Light Range's values past level 14.

## Verdict

**T012 Rev 4** (§2.2), **H058 updated** with the prose branch. The card layer's two ladders are now each printed twice, the
mastery law is reproduced on a second page type, and the stone census is confirmed card by card. For the plan nothing
numerical moves — the same eight masteries, the same 7 000-stone unlock layer — which is the point of a double-source pass.
**Next:** finish the per-card census (the other 28 pages), then the uncited `wiki/currency/*` pages
(`currency-bits`, `currency-gems`, `currency-keys`, `currency-power-stones`, `currency-tokens`).

# 2026-11-02 - Session 96 (iteration 105: the card census closes and the special-card pages print the lab layer)

## Asked

Two things: (a) does the mastery law from §2.1 survive on **all** per-card pages, not just the 8 pulled in iteration 104,
and (b) what do the nine special-card pages (`death-ray`, `nuke`, `second-wind`, …) under `wiki/card/` carry — they were
never cited and their table shape is the lab shape (`Level | Time | Cost | Value`).

## Sources

Tower Hub, all last verified **2026-11-01**: 15 card pages (`cards-area-of-effect` … `cards-wave-accelerator`) and 10
special/preset pages (`death-ray`, `demon-mode`, `energy-net`, `energy-shield`, `nuke`, `nuke-vs-slow-aura-mastery`,
`plasma-cannon`, `second-wind`, `slow-aura`, `card-presets`); parsed with `tools/card-page-parse.ps1` (upgraded: strips
`x` and trailing `s`) and `tools/page-seg.ps1` (new). Note: `tools/uncited-pages.ps1` now matches the bare slug too —
the five `wiki/currency/*` pages it flagged are in fact already cited in T022 §Sources (bare-slug form).

## What was established

**A. The census is closed for the standard cards (23 pages, 22 with both tables).** Every card ladder matches §1 cell for
cell; the five non-arithmetic shapes that surface (`Area of Effect` +3 ×5 then +5; `Damage`/`Health` +0.5 then +0.4 ×5;
`Intro Sprint` +10 ×4 then +20 ×2; plus `Wave Skip`/`Super Tower`) are **exactly §1's “seven exceptions”**, so no new
break. 22 pages print stone costs; all fall in the four §2.1 buckets (750 ×10, 1 000 ×8 + Package Chance, 1 250 ×2 of 3,
500 ×2 done) — only 9 mastery rows + the `Cells` prediction stay single-print.

**B. The mastery law is universal as `v(k) = base + (k + 1) × s`** over all 22 two-table pages: base = 1 for the `×` rows
(`Coins`/`Attack Speed` 1+0.03(k+1), `Extra Orb` +0.04, `Damage`/`Health Regen` +0.4, `Health`/`Range` +0.2), base = 0
for plain %-rows, and the printed offset where a base is named (`Wave Accelerator` 100+10(k+1), `Wave Skip` 5+5(k+1) —
even the §2.1 “only break” fits the general form; it only breaks the 10 % ratio). `Ultimate Crit` prints the rounded
⅓ ladder on **both** tables (0.3/0.7/1.0/1.3/1.7/2.0/2.3/2.7/3.0/3.3 = `(k+1)/3` to 1 dp) — §2.1's rounding call
double-sourced.

**C. The special-card pages are a lab print.** `second-wind` reproduces T006's `Second Wind Blast` (25/50/75/100 % at
1.8 M/3.0 M/4.5 M/75 M) and `Recharge Second Wind` (2 000→400 at 550 B→3.60 T) cell for cell; its four printed times sum
to T006's Σ 750 000 s ⇒ `Time` **per-level**, third confirmation of the H050 reading on a third page type. New prints:
`demon-mode`/`nuke` value ladder **1 500→300** (same cost column), the whole 30-level **Double Death Ray** lab
(**T3 W300** gate, value = level number, Σ **1 097.15 B ≈ 1.10 T** coins, Σ **21 415.5 h = 892.3 d**, 79.7 % of the time
in levels 21–30), `Energy Shield Extra Hit` **40 B + 850 B / 43.98 d**, and the named 5-level presets ladder
(Σ **35.35 T**, 34 d 4 h).

**D. The mastery product is printed.** `nuke-vs-slow-aura-mastery` pins `Slow Aura` = 1+0.05(k+1), `Nuke` = 1+0.1(k+1)
on partial rows and states “Both at 9 … **×3.00 combined**” = 1.50 × 2.00 — the §2.1 product law (270 = 5×6×9) verified
on a second page; its timer table is fully multiplicative (2.0→3.0→4.0→6.0 s pause, 150→100→75→50 hits/5 min).

**E. Three mechanics prints:** `Slow Aura` slows Basic/Fast/Tank inside range only and stacks **multiplicatively** with
Chrono Field and the −40 %/×2.5 perk; `Energy Net` and `Plasma Cannon` projectile speed scales with **Attack Speed** and
ignores `Light Speed Shot(s)`; `Death Ray` clears nothing inside a Protector radius and cannot kill Bosses ✓ T006's
immunity list.

## Next open question

Whether the special-card pages’ per-level `Time` ladder matches the in-game lab panel exactly (device read; the 892.3 d
Double Death Ray Σ rests on the per-level reading that the 4-level `Blast` table proves by summation). Device reads
unchanged: **H060**, **H050**, **H058** (two candidate panel reads), **H059**, T010 §8 skews, T024 §6, the 98 % vs 99 %
`Defense %` skew, Light Range's values past level 14. Also open: the last 9 single-print stone rows (three 750s, five
1 000s, `Plasma Cannon` 1 250) and `Cells` = 1 000 by prediction.

## Verdict

**T012 Rev 5** (§2.2 census + new §2.3, three checklist items, three falsifiers, two Sources bullets). The card layer is
now fully censused and every printed ladder is law-backed; the special-card pages became a fourth print of the
Card-Research labs. For the plan: the **stop-the-lab rule** now has a numeric case (79.7 % of Double Death Ray's time is
in its last 10 levels). **Next:** the remaining `wiki/system/*` and `wiki/uw/*` uncited slugs via the fixed
`tools/uncited-pages.ps1`, then a first device-read pass on H058's two panel reads.

law, all within minute-truncation). Σ one lab = **116.25 q coins / 433 881 270 s = 5 021.8 d**; all eight = **930.00 q /
40 174 d**. The stone track: “going from level k to k+1 costs **3k + 15** stones” ⇒ Σ(k=0…68) = 3·2 346 + 15·69 =
**8 073** ✓ the page's printed total, 69 levels. Efficiency law: **1 + 69 + 30 = 100 %** (base + stone levels + lab
levels). Printed substat **hard caps**: **98 % defense** (settles this repo's 98-vs-99 skew toward Tower Hub's **98**),
**90 %** Chrono Field slow, **150 s** Wall rebuild, **7 s** Shockwave Frequency, **37 s** Inner Land Mine cooldown.

printed (Â§8.7). The Generator table re-reads identical to Â§8.3 (a clean second pass over the same 13 rows âœ“).

**B. The rarity-pool law (new).** Each row begins at exactly one rarity, and the histogram is
**Common 23 Â· Rare 9 Â· Epic 24 Â· Legendary 16 Â· Mythic 1** (Î£ **73** âœ“), so the number of *possible* sub-stats per module
rarity is cumulative: **23 / 32 / 56 / 72 / 73 / 73**. That is the Â§8.2 cap law turned into a counting rule â€” a Common
module has only 23 candidate sub-stats, a Legendary+ module 72 â€” and it is a one-tap check (count the `â„¹` list).

**C. Shape laws.** **24 of 73 rows are exactly arithmetic** (4 Cannon / 2 Armor / 7 Generator / 11 Core); the rest are
piecewise (`Attack Speed` +0.2 Ã—3 then +2 Ã—2; `Cash / Wave` +20/+50/+100/+300/+500). **Seven value vectors cover 22
rows**, including two new coincidences: the three `Free â€¦ Upgrade` rows share their ladder with **`Black Hole - Size`**

(8/15/25/50/100/250 âœ“ already known).

**D. Reroll economics ranked.** Ancestral Ã· Common per row: `Defense Absolute` **66.7**, `Cash / Wave` **33.3**, Core
damage **31.25**, `Cash Bonus` **25**, `Coins / Wave` **17.5**, `Coins / Kill Bonus` and the Free-Upgrade trio **6**,
`Interest / Wave` + Level Skips **4**, `Package Chance` **3**, cooldowns **2â€“2.2**. Practical rule added: spend the
10â€“40-shard reroll on the **high-ratio** rows (Cash/Coins per Wave) and push module rarity, not on `Package Chance`.

**E. One cooldown escape hatch.** Every `âˆ’N s` row starts at Legendary **except `Poison Swamp - Cooldown`**, printed from
**Rare** (`âˆ’2/âˆ’4/âˆ’6/âˆ’8/âˆ’10`) â€” so below Tier 11 the only UW cooldown a module can carry is Poison Swampâ€™s, while T008â€™s
Golden Tower / Chrono Field cooldown targets need Legendary+ modules.

**F. Model migration, dated.** The fandomâ€™s v0.24 list has 11 rows; four of them (Armor Penetration, Coin Drop Rate,
Kill Coins, Energy â€” plus a `Wave Skip` 2â†’50 that is today a *workshop* line) are absent from the 73-row print â‡’ the
fandom table is the **v0.24-era** model (see T005â€™s timeline). Where both exist they agree to the cell
(`Coin Bonus +10 % â†’ +50 %` = `Cash Bonus` Common **0.1** â†’ Legendary **0.5** âœ“).
